## Review

**7 out of 10, Effective.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck has a clear route (who we are, what we do, how we do it, strategy, performance, governance) with consistent teal and orange identity, strong photography, readable section dividers, and an investor-ready financial close with a clear dividend chart (54). Weaknesses are consequential: many body slides carry very small text (10 to 12, 27 to 28, 32, 44), some slides are largely filler or leave blank space (14, 16, 17), and text sits on busy photos with weak contrast (12 to 13 labels on tiles, 22 to 24 banners, 31). Slide 7 packs three charts and many tiny figures without a takeaway, and slide 54 clips the last two bar labels and has a title that does not match the free cash flow bars.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the consistent section dividers, the strong editorial photography on the opener and chapter slides, the two-line title with a message subtitle, the doughnut plus product-list pairing on 9 and 20, and the dividend and free cash flow chart on 54.

**Most useful repair:** Raise the smallest type on text-heavy slides (10, 12, 23, 27, 32, 44), put solid panels behind text over photos (11, 12, 28, 31), fix the clipped labels on 54, and fill or tighten the sparse slides 14, 16 and 17.

**What would change the score:** Split or simplify slide 7 into one clear message per chart, and add a clear takeaway to the performance slides so the argument is not left to the reader.

_Confidence medium. 60 of 60 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

The deck opens with a title slide and a short 'Who we are' section covering Givaudan's brand video, global footprint of production sites, creation centres, locations and employees, and a split of sales across its Taste & Wellbeing and Fragrance & Beauty businesses. It then moves through each business in turn, showing product categories, growth drivers to 2030, differentiated solutions and commercial strategy, followed by a 'How we do it' section on customer success, the research-driven innovation process and future-proofing through digital tools. A strategy section sets out the 2030 targets, growth drivers and purpose ambitions for nature, people and communities. The financial part reviews performance across the 2021 to 2025 cycle, half-year 2026 results, dividend history, debt maturities and the 2026 outlook. Governance pages introduce the Executive Committee and Board, and a reporting section and disclaimer close the deck. Slides worth studying include the sales-mix slide with its repeated tiles, the business-activity slides that pair a product list with a short positioning line, and the long-run dividend and free cash flow bar chart.

## Questions about this deck

**How many pages does the Givaudan September 2026 investor presentation have?**

The deck has 60 pages. Slide 1 is the title slide, and the deck closes with a reporting section and disclaimer.

**What sales split does the Givaudan presentation show for Fragrance & Beauty?**

Slide 20 shows Consumer Products at 61%, Fine Fragrance at 23%, and Fragrance Ingredients & Active Beauty at 16% of Fragrance & Beauty. Slide 7 also lists these categories within the portfolio breakdown.

**What growth drivers does Givaudan identify for 2030 in its presentation?**

Slide 11 lists five growth drivers to 2030: Health consciousness, Better choices, Affordable convenience, Fast growing segments and regions, and Sensory maximisation. Slide 12 shows the same drivers alongside Givaudan's differentiated solutions.

**How much did Givaudan spend on R&D in 2025 according to its deck?**

Slide 29 states CHF 551mln R&D spend in 2025, which it gives as 7.4% of sales. The same slide also reports 65 creation and research centres and more than 5,000 active patents.

Evolving customer needs

Givaudan
6

**Layout**

Centered text stack at the top: title with subtitle, then a bold statement and a regular sentence, all center-aligned. Two photos sit side by side, full width, edge to edge, in the lower middle, each with a white caption centered on it. Footer row: left-aligned date line at the left margin, Givaudan wordmark centered, page number at the right.

**Visuals**

Two photographs side by side, touching at the center and bleeding to the left and right slide edges. Left: an outdoor garden dinner. A bearded man in a mustard-yellow knitted beanie and denim shirt laughs beside a blonde woman in a pink sweater who is eating a sandwich. A smiling bearded man in glasses and a striped top is partly cropped at the left edge. A table in the foreground holds glass bottles, glasses and plates of food. Background of green plants and bamboo fencing. Warm, soft natural light, greens, yellows and pinks. Right: a young woman with dark hair, wrapped in a white towel turban, looks into a mirror and touches her lips with a finger. She wears a black floral top. A blurred white towel is in the left foreground. Warm brown tones, a white wall with a mirror frame and fairy lights at the right. Both photos have a slight darkening so the white captions are readable. The Givaudan wordmark is plain black text at the bottom center.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background. Black text (#000000) throughout, white (#FFFFFF) captions over the photos. Typeface is Jost, a geometric sans-serif. Title is SemiBold at about 5.2% of slide height, subtitle Regular at 5%. Statement is SemiBold and the sentence Regular, both about 3.7%. Captions are SemiBold 3.7%. Footer is Regular at about 1.5%. No shapes, lines, shadows or gradients.

### Slide 7

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Uses a dense multi-panel layout with consistent colour coding to compare geography, product mix and customer type side by side.
- Review: Slide 7 packs three charts and many tiny figures without a takeaway, and slide 54 clips the last two bar labels and has a title that does not match the free cash flow bars.
- What would change the score: Split or simplify slide 7 into one clear message per chart, and add a clear takeaway to the performance slides so the argument is not left to the reader.

7

**Layout**

White slide with centered two-line title at top. Below, three columns, Customers (left), Products (middle) and Geography (right), separated by two thin vertical orange lines at about x=19% and x=54%. Column headings are centered over each column. Footer has footnote and document line at bottom left, Givaudan wordmark at bottom center, page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Customers: two vertical stacked bars with light grey tops. Left bar is 59% in teal #277E8E at the bottom (Local and Regional). Right bar is 41% in blue #006EA3 at the bottom (Global). Products: two sets of concentric partial-ring (radial bar) charts, each with a thin teal vertical bracket line left of it. Taste & Wellbeing (49%) has four arcs: outer teal 37%, blue 36%, yellow 15%, orange 12%. Fragrance & Beauty (51%) has three arcs: outer teal 61%, blue 23%, yellow 16%. Geography: three pie charts (Sales, Employees, Sites) with square-marker legends of EAME, APAC, NOAM, LATAM. Sales pie: EAME 42% light teal #35A2B6, APAC 24% blue, NOAM 23% yellow, LATAM 11% orange. Employees pie: EAME 47% dark teal #277E8E, APAC 21% blue, NOAM 18% yellow, LATAM 14% orange. Sites pie: EAME 78 dark teal, APAC 47 blue, NOAM 24 yellow, LATAM 18 orange. Two stacked bars at bottom right with light grey tops: Sales in mature markets 51% teal, Sales in high growth markets 49% blue. Givaudan wordmark in footer.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background. Black titles and text in Jost, geometric sans-serif. Title 'What we do' is semibold at about 5.2% of slide height, subtitle regular at 5%. Column headings are semibold at about 3.7%. Accent colours are teal #35A2B6, dark teal #277E8E, blue #006EA3, yellow #FAC400, orange #ED6E00, light grey #D9D9D9. Orange divider lines #ED6E00. White semibold labels sit inside the chart segments. Flat style with no shadows or gradients.

**Layout**

Full-bleed photograph with centred white text: small heading near the top centre, large title at the vertical middle, subtitle just below it. The Givaudan wordmark sits at bottom centre and a tiny page number is at the bottom right.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed photograph, slightly darkened by a 10% black overlay. Close-up of people sitting on the floor at a picnic, shot from above and at an angle. On the left, a person in a white and navy striped shirt with a cream scarf holds out a white marble serving board with two hands. On the board are four glasses of frothy milky tea or latte, each dusted with cocoa. A hand reaches in from the right. In the background are colourful macarons in a wooden bowl, a red tray, spoons, and a plate with yellow and white sweets in the bottom centre. Pink and mustard blankets cover the floor. The lighting is warm and soft with a shallow depth of field. Dominant colours are cream, beige, navy, pink and mustard. A white Givaudan logo wordmark appears at the bottom centre.

**Design**

Background: Full-bleed photograph of a picnic scene with tea glasses on a marble board, under a 10% black overlay (white base fill #FFFFFF underneath). Photo background with a 10% black tint. All text is white (#FFFFFF) in Jost SemiBold, a geometric sans-serif. The title is about 10% of the slide height and the heading and subtitle about 5%. The page number is tiny black Jost Regular. There are no lines, shadows or gradients.

Large donut chart titled in the centre 'Taste & Wellbeing' with four segments clockwise from top: Savoury 37% (dark blue), Beverages 36% (teal), Sweet Goods 15% (yellow), Dairy 12% (orange). Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Category headings semibold about 3% in the segment colours: blue #006EA3, teal #35A2B6, yellow #FAC400, orange #ED6E00. Bullets regular about 2.2% black with small round bullets. Chart labels white semibold, percentages about 5.2%, labels about 3%. Flat design, no shadows or gradients.

### Slide 10

**Text**

Customer-centric growth driven by core strengths
Deep consumer understanding, creative excellence and customer proximity
Decode consumer / sensory needs
Create winning / solutions
Strengthen partner / leadership
Industry-leading consumer understanding powered by insights, foresight and sensory science
Deep science, technology and creative expertise transforming insights into differentiated products
Deep customer relationships enabled by the industry’s broadest flavour creation and application footprint
Consumer insights & foresight
Sensory science
TasteTrek® programmes
Deep Science & Technology capabilities
Flavour Academy nurturing world-class flavourists
Co-creation and open innovation ecosystem
Digital & AI-enabled capabilities
Deepest customer relationships in the industry
Widest local flavour creation & application footprint
Front-end open innovation ecosystem
Partner to global champions and fast-rising local & regional players
CREATING TANGIBLE VALUE, STRENGTHENING RELATIONSHIPS, AND DRIVING DIFFERENTIATION
2026 - Investor presentation – updated September 2026
Givaudan
10

**Layout**

Centered two-line title at top. Three equal columns (about 29.5% wide each, gaps of about 2%) each with a photo banner carrying white overlaid heading, a short description, a thin grey divider and a bullet list. An orange tagline with grey lines at both ends sits below the columns. Footer has a left date line, a centered Givaudan wordmark and a right page number.

**Visuals**

Three photo banners, each 29.5% x 18.3% of the slide. Left: close-up of a lime wedge, with yellow-green flesh and blurred golden-brown background, warm light, tinted slightly dark. Middle: a hand picking up small sample vials or bottle caps from a tray, on a blurred grey and black background, with a grey tint. Right: two smiling people in warm light, a woman at left and a man at right in profile, wearing yellow and white checked blankets or scarves, with a dark brown background and an amber tint. Thin grey horizontal divider lines under each description. At the tagline, thin grey horizontal lines with small grey dot end caps at far left and far right. Givaudan wordmark text in the footer.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in black #000000 Jost SemiBold, about 5.2% of slide height; subtitle in Jost Regular black, about 5%. ### Slide 11

**Text**

Capturing the biggest growth opportunities by 2030
Leveraging core strengths to meet consumer trends and customer needs
GROWTH DRIVERS
Health consciousness
Better choices
Affordable convenience
Fast growing segments and regions
Sensory maximisation
CONSUMER NEEDS AND CUSTOMER EXPECTATIONS
Health benefits
Less sugar, salt and fat; more protein and fibre
Replace commodities, high taste impact at low dosage
Access to fast-growing opportunities and premium, mouthwatering new products
Bolder, more authentic, delectable, taste — wins Gen Z and Gen Alpha
WITH UNCOMPROMISED TASTE
Givaudan
2026 - Investor presentation – updated September 2026
11

**Layout**

Centered two-line title block at top (bold title over lighter subtitle). Below, three centered blue uppercase section headings, each flanked by thin grey horizontal rules with dot end caps. A row of five equal photo tiles sits under the first heading, and five text columns with thin vertical dividers sit under the second. Footer has date text at left, Givaudan wordmark at centre and page number at right.

**Visuals**

Five photo tiles in a row, each with a white semibold overlay label at upper left. 1) Health consciousness: close-up of a person in a pale sage-green jacket in motion, with a birch trunk and green moss at lower left, muted green tones. 2) Better choices: a hand in a pink top reaching for a pink-glazed doughnut on a plate with a chocolate doughnut, on a blue and white gingham cloth, with a green drink glass at top. 3) Affordable convenience: a hand holding a tall glass of pale orange drink with a grapefruit wedge and a sugared rim, against a dark denim and plaid shirt, dark navy tones. 4) Fast growing segments and regions: golden-brown baked pastries or biscuits on a grey-blue tray, shallow depth of field, warm brown against cool grey. 5) Sensory maximisation: macro of red-orange spices and seeds with glossy droplets, saturated red and orange. Thin grey horizontal rules with small grey circular end caps run at three levels (y about 25%, 57% and 85%), interrupted by the headings. Thin light-grey vertical divider lines at the left of each of the five text columns. The Givaudan wordmark is in the footer centre.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title black #000000 in Jost SemiBold at about 5.2% of slide height, subtitle black Jost Regular at about 5%. Section headings are blue #006EA3 in Jost SemiBold uppercase with wide letter-spacing at about 2.6%. Tile labels are white Jost SemiBold at about 4.1%. Column body text is black Jost Regular at about 2.6%. Footer text is black Jost Regular at about 1.5%. Rules are light grey #BFBFBF with dot end caps. Tiles have square corners, no shadows and no gradients.

**Layout**

White slide with centred two-line title at top. Below, a 'GROWTH DRIVERS' divider line with centred blue heading, then a row of five equal photo tiles with white overlaid titles, then a 'GIVAUDAN’S DIFFERENTIATED SOLUTIONS' divider and five bullet columns aligned under the tiles, separated by thin vertical grey lines. Footer text bottom left, page number bottom right, 'Givaudan' wordmark text bottom centre.

**Visuals**

Two horizontal thin grey divider lines, each with a small grey dot at both ends, interrupted in the centre by blue heading text. Five photo tiles in a row: 1) a person in a light grey-green jacket running or walking outdoors, green foliage and a birch trunk at lower left, muted tones; 2) a pink-sleeved hand reaching for a pink-glazed donut on a plate with chocolate donuts, on a blue and white gingham picnic cloth, a green drink at top; 3) a hand holding a glass of pale orange cocktail with a grapefruit slice, person in denim shirt, dark background; 4) close-up of golden-brown sugar-dusted pastry/fried dough pieces on a grey-blue tray, cool muted tones; 5) close-up of red-orange spices and chilli flakes with seeds, saturated red. Thin vertical grey separator lines between bullet columns. The 'Givaudan' wordmark at the bottom centre is plain text. Bullets are small black dots.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white background. Title in black #000000 Jost SemiBold with the subtitle in Jost Regular, about 5% of slide height. Section headings in blue #006EA3 Jost SemiBold, uppercase with wide letter-spacing, about 2.6%. Tile titles white Jost SemiBold about 4% of height over photos. Body bullets black Jost Regular about 2.6%. Grey #BBBBBB thin lines. No shadows, no rounded corners.

**Review notes**

**Text**

Strengthening the next chapter
Leverage strengths and sharpen focus to accelerate growth
Structural market growth
Large, resilient market with accelerating GLP-1, reformulation and natural-colour tailwinds; higher growth concentrated in health, functionality and natural solutions.
Demographic shifts in emerging markets continue to expand demand for affordable solutions.

Further strengthen leadership in core Flavour & Taste Solutions.

Portfolio optimisation opportunities

Givaudan
18

**Layout**

Split slide: full-height photo fills the left 38% of the slide; the right side is white with a centered two-tier title near the top and four left-aligned text sections stacked below, separated by thin grey rules. Page number sits at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed photograph on the left, cropped to the left edge and full height: overhead view of a hand-stretched raw pizza dough on a metal peel over a wooden board, topped with crumbled white cheese, grated cheese, grilled artichoke pieces and halved red grapes. A hand at top left pulls the dough edge and another hand at bottom left holds the peel. Warm natural light, dominant colours cream, beige, tan wood and dark red. No overlay or tint. Three thin light-grey horizontal divider lines separate the text sections on the right. Text 'Givaudan' appears as a small wordmark-like text near the bottom, no graphic logo.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Titles and body in black #000000; accent blue #006EA3 for key phrases in semibold. Typeface Jost (geometric sans-serif): semibold for the main title and section headings, regular for the subtitle and body. Title about 5.2% of slide height, section headings about 3% and body about 2.6%. Thin light-grey rules #BFBFBF, no shadows, gradients or rounded corners.

### Slide 19

**Text**

Our business activities
Fragrance & Beauty
Perfume lives and brings beauty to the world

19

**Layout**

Full-bleed photograph with centred white text. A small title 'Our business activities' sits top centre; the large heading 'Fragrance & Beauty' and a subtitle line are centred in the middle of the slide. A 'Givaudan' footer is centred at the bottom and a small page number sits at the bottom right.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed photograph of a Provence lavender field, shot at low level from within the rows. Blurred purple lavender sprigs fill the left and right foreground edges, framing a sharp central row that recedes along a brown earth path toward the centre. Behind it are green hills with rocky outcrops and farmland, and a pale blue-grey mountain ridge under a hazy blue-grey sky. Soft natural daylight; dominant colours are purple, olive green, grey-blue. A black overlay at 10% opacity darkens the photo slightly. The 'Givaudan' footer is probably a logo image, a small white wordmark at the bottom centre.

**Design**

Background: Full-bleed lavender field photograph under a 10% black overlay (white base fill beneath). Photo background with 10% black tint; all main text is white #FFFFFF in Jost SemiBold (geometric sans-serif). Title and subtitle are about 5.2% of slide height; the main heading is about 10.2%. The page number is small, regular weight, black #000000. No lines, shadows or gradients.

Footer has the date line at left, the Givaudan wordmark at centre and the page number at right.

**Visuals**

Donut chart titled Fragrance & Beauty in its centre (white hole). Three segments starting at 12 o'clock and going clockwise: Consumer Products 61% in dark blue #006EA3 (right and bottom), Fragrance Ingredients & Active Beauty 16% in teal #35A2B6 (lower left), Fine Fragrance 23% in orange #ED6E00 (upper left). Each segment has a white label and white percentage, with a white icon: a hand being washed with bubbles in the blue segment, a perfume bottle in the orange segment, and a dropper bottle with sparkles in the teal segment. A thin vertical teal-blue line separates the statement from the text column. The Givaudan wordmark sits in the footer centre.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Black titles in Jost SemiBold (title) and Jost Regular (subtitle), geometric sans-serif. The title is about 5.2% of slide height. Body statement in black semibold at about 3.7%. Headings are coloured to match the chart segments: blue #006EA3, teal #35A2B6, orange #ED6E00, semibold at about 3%. Bullets in black Jost Regular at about 2.2%. Chart labels are white and bold, with percentages at about 5.2%. Flat fills, no shadows or gradients.

**Visuals**

Four photos in a row, each 21.5% wide by 26.1% high, plain rectangles with no border. 1) Young man with short dark curly hair in a white t-shirt, applying white dabs of cream to his face with both hands, light grey-blue bright background. 2) Close-up of a woman's wrist and a hand spraying perfume from a glass bottle, cream chunky knit sleeve, soft white background. 3) Flat lay with a silver-capped bottle of amber-yellow oil, red berries, wood chips, dark spice seeds and reed sticks on a white ribbed surface. 4) Macro of a pale cream flower with radiating filaments, an orange-peach crystal-like object and water bubbles, in peach, orange and grey tones. ### Slide 22

**Text**

Technologies and unique ingredients
Designed to enhance fragrance performance and elevate the wellbeing of consumers
Unique ingredients
Our exclusive portfolio of captive molecules developed by our team of scientists, and are designed to deliver outstanding results in fragrances.
Malodour control
We have several mechanisms for odour reduction, prevention, elimination, and protection, with over twenty technologies, tailored suit to consumers evolving needs.
Bloom
Cutting-edge solutions which enhance and prolong the moment of bloom, bringing reassurance and comfort to consumers.
Enhanced wellbeing
Leading solutions to enhance consumers wellbeing, designed with intimate knowledge of the special relationship between scent and emotion.
Long-lasting freshness
We provide solutions to deliver long-lasting freshness, including our iconic PlanetCaps™ range, bringing to consumers confidence and comfort across categories.
Health and hygiene
Solutions to support health and hygiene are designed to address bacteria, fungi and viruses, and instil reassurance in the busy lives of our consumers.
2026 - Investor presentation – updated September 2026
Givaudan
22

**Layout**

Centered two-line title block at top (bold title, lighter subtitle wrapping to two lines). Below, a three-row by two-column grid of text blocks with a photo in the centre of each row pair: left text blocks are right-aligned and end against the left photos; right text blocks are left-aligned and start beside the right photos. Footer has date text at left, Givaudan wordmark at centre, page number at right.

**Visuals**

Six rectangular uncropped photos in two central columns, each beside its text block. Row 1 left: abstract turquoise and teal swirling liquid or paint with orange and white streaks. Row 1 right: white sheets hanging on a washing line among trees, dappled sunlight, warm golden-green tones. Row 2 left: white linens and duvets on a white metal drying rack, soft light. Row 2 right: silhouetted woman holding up a sheer fabric in warm golden sunset light, muted brown tones. Row 3 left: soft pastel peach and cream beach scene with a gentle wave breaking. Row 3 right: woman in a white t-shirt seen from behind folding a beige towel above a white perforated laundry basket, white towel hanging above, bright airy light. Givaudan wordmark text in the footer, no other icons.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background. All text black #000000 in Jost, a geometric sans-serif. Title is Jost SemiBold at about 5.2% of slide height; subtitle is Jost Regular at about 5%. Section headings are Jost SemiBold at about 3.7%, body text is Jost Regular at about 2.2%, footer text at about 1.5%. No lines, shadows, gradients or rounded corners.

### Slide 28

**Text**

A unique business model driven by our purpose
Creating for happier, healthier lives with love for nature
Sustainability
Mastering complexity and volatility
Compliance and regulation
• Ambitious sustainability goals create significant innovation opportunities
• Transitioning from non-renewable to renewable resources
• Leveraging white biotechnology
• Committed to supporting customers on their sustainability journey
• Strong track record in operational excellence, financial performance resilience, and continuity
• Managing over 10,000 raw materials through a sophisticated supply chain with 2,800 suppliers
• Delivering 143,800 made-to-order products just in time to around 12,000 customers on a global scale
• Enable customers to stay ahead of increasing regulatory pressure through innovative solutions
• Strong, sticky relationships with customers due to high switching costs
• Intellectual property and proprietary formulae owned by Givaudan enhance customer loyalty
2026 - Investor presentation – updated September 2026
Givaudan
28

**Layout**

Centered two-line title at top. Below it, a row of three equal adjacent photo panels (each 25% wide) centered with white overlay headings. Under each photo a bulleted list in a column aligned with the panel. Footer has the date line at left, Givaudan wordmark at center and the page number at right.

**Visuals**

Three adjacent photographs, full-width of their panels, no gaps. Left: close-up of purple lavender flowers on slender stems against a blurred green grass background, natural daylight. Middle: a dense pile of glossy red berries or pink peppercorns with a deep crimson tint, close crop, filling the frame. Right: a person in a soft cream/white loose blouse and trousers, hands clasped at the lower right, bright airy light, with a blurred green background at the bottom, washed-out pale tones. Givaudan wordmark logo in the footer, black.

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background. Title in black semi-bold geometric sans-serif (Jost SemiBold #000000), subtitle in black Jost Regular, heading labels in white Jost SemiBold over the photos, body text in black Jost Regular with small round bullets. Title about 5.2% of slide height, subtitle 5%, panel headings about 4.1-4.4%, body about 2%, footer about 1.5%. No shadows, borders or gradients.

Footer has the presentation line at left, the Givaudan wordmark at centre and the page number at far right.

**Visuals**

Five photographs in the grid, each about 23% wide, with a thin pale-blue hairline border around the grid. Top-left: blurred close-up of lab wash bottles and glassware behind glass, in green-grey tones. Top-right: two men in white lab coats looking at equipment in a lab, one holding a black folder, in cool grey-white light. Centre: close-up of a person with glasses and red nail polish using tweezers over rows of small glass bottles with yellow and amber liquids. Bottom-left: a gloved hand holding a small beaker over a dark wooden table with glass bottles, in warm brown tones. Bottom-right: a young woman with long brown hair smelling a perfumer's paper strip, in a blurred blue lab setting. The Givaudan wordmark is in the footer. The four stat cells are white with large teal numerals.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background. The title is black Jost SemiBold, about 5.2% of slide height. The subtitle is black Jost Regular, about 5%. Stat numerals are teal #35A2B6 Jost SemiBold, about 6.5%, with smaller teal CHF and mln at 3.7%. Captions are black Jost Regular, about 1.84%. Footer text is black Jost Regular, about 1.5%. There are no shadows or gradients, and the corners are square.

Title near the top, centred. Below it a blue heading band with thin rules and end dots, then a row of five equal portrait photo panels with white labels, a second heading band beneath, and a footer with the date line at left, the Givaudan wordmark at centre and the page number at right.

**Visuals**

Five portrait photographs side by side, each about 17.7% wide and 52% tall, with small gaps. 1) An older grey-haired man in a blue linen shirt smells a small sample vial in a lab with shelves of small red-capped bottles; warm, soft light. 2) A green plant in the foreground with a white and black robotic arm in the background; teal-blue, cool, bright tones. 3) Two hands raised and touching palm to palm, one light-skinned and one dark-skinned, against a soft pink-lilac sky; muted pastel. 4) A smiling worker in a black hard hat with a G logo, safety glasses, beard net and beige coveralls, arms crossed, in a factory with pipes; warm, muted. 5) Close-up of weathered hands planting a small green seedling in dark soil, with a thin teal string; earthy brown and green. White text labels are overlaid on the photos. Two thin light-grey horizontal rules with round grey dots at the outer ends flank each blue heading, one above the photos and one below. The Givaudan wordmark sits at bottom centre in black.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white background. Title is black Jost SemiBold, about 5.2% of slide height. Upper heading is in blue #006EA3 and the lower heading in teal #35A2B6, both uppercase, widely letter-spaced Jost SemiBold at about 2.6% of height. Photo labels are white Jost SemiBold at about 4.4% of height, centred. Footer text is small black Jost Regular. Rules are thin grey #BBBBBB with dot terminals. No shadows or gradients, and the photos have square corners.

**Layout**

White slide with a centered title near the top. Below it, five equal columns (left edges at about 3.3%, 22%, 40.6%, 59.2%, 77.8%), each with a bold heading, a lighter subheading, a bullet list and a square-ish photo at the bottom. Footer: left-aligned deck label at bottom left, Givaudan wordmark centered at the bottom, page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Five photographs in a row, each about 16.8% wide and 29% tall, boxed with no border or overlay. 1) Creation: an older man with grey hair in a light blue button-up shirt smelling a small glass perfume vial, standing in front of shelves of small bottles with maroon caps; soft warm light, pale blue and cream tones. 2) Science & Technology: a white robotic arm dispensing liquid onto a green young plant in a bright laboratory; teal and green blurred background, cool light. 3) Customer excellence: a light-skinned hand and a dark-skinned hand pressed together palm to palm, raised against a soft pastel pink and lavender sky; pale tones. 4) Operational excellence: a smiling worker in a black hard hat, hairnet beard cover and tan coveralls with reflective yellow stripes, arms crossed, standing in an industrial plant with pipes and tanks; warm beige and grey tones. 5) Sustainability: close-up of weathered hands planting a small green seedling in dark brown soil, with a thin teal string; natural daylight, green and brown tones. The Givaudan wordmark in thin black sans-serif text is at the footer centre.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white background. All text is black (#000000) in Jost (geometric sans-serif). Title is Jost SemiBold at about 5.2% of slide height. Column headings are Jost SemiBold at about 2.6%. Subheadings are Jost Regular at about 2.2%. Bullets are Jost Regular at about 1.84% with round black bullets and hanging indents. The footer is about 1.5%. No lines, shadows or gradients; photos have square corners.

**Layout**

Centered two-line title at top. A horizontal chevron timeline band spans the slide at about 41-47% height with 12 year labels from 2014 to 2026 (2022 is skipped). Taste & Wellbeing acquisition logos sit above the band and Fragrance & Beauty logos below, each with a descriptor caption beneath. Rotated vertical category labels sit at the left margin. Two outlined KPI boxes sit bottom left. Footer has the presentation line at left, the Givaudan wordmark in the centre and the page number at right.

**Visuals**

A band of 12 teal chevron arrows pointing right, with the last one forming an arrowhead. A thin black vertical axis line at the left separates the two categories, running from about 21% to 69% height. Company logos above the band: Vika (green, 'the base for success'), Naturex (green wordmark with leaf), Golden Frog (red text with green frog and 'GF' badge), Spicetec (orange/brown wordmark with swirl icon), Activ International (blue wordmark with droplet), Grupo Centroflora (blue text with green leaf mark), a red circular seal logo (Specialty Ingredients), DDW The Color House (black text with blue, white and red diamond). Logos below the band: Soliance (grey/brown wordmark with green leaf), Induchem companies (grey/orange wordmark), Expressions Parfumées (black bold text), Albert Vieille (blue flower mark with brown text), Drom Fragrances (black bold wordmark), Fragrance Oils (black swirl rose emblem), AMSilk (purple A mark), Indena (black/green wordmark), Alderys (blue), Custom Essence (black text with green clover), Myrissi (black text), Amyris (purple bold wordmark), b.kolor (grey with blue dot), Vollmens (grey V mark), Belle Aire Creations (black script), Eurofragance (purple/pink wordmark with pink spark). Two KPI boxes with thin teal outline and white fill.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Titles in black Jost: 'Our history' semibold, subtitle regular. Teal #35A2B6 chevrons with white Jost regular year labels. Black Jost captions. KPI boxes have a 2px teal #35A2B6 outline, square corners, no shadow, black Arial text with bold values. Flat style, no gradients.

34

**Layout**

Centered two-line title at top. Below, a 4-column by 2-row grid of executive profiles, each with a thin grey rule on top, teal name, black role and bullet lines at left and a square portrait at right. Underneath, a bulleted list at left and a teal-outlined box at right; footer with left-aligned text, centered Givaudan wordmark and page number at right.

**Visuals**

Eight square headshot photographs of executives, each in business attire against a light grey/white studio background, head-and-shoulders crops. Row 1: Christian Stammkoetter (man with swept brown hair, dark suit, white shirt, smiling slightly), Stewart Harris (smiling man with receding hair, dark suit), Maurizio Volpi (man with wavy dark grey hair, dark suit, grey background), Antoine Khalil (bald man, dark suit, light blue shirt). Row 2: Simon Halle-Smith (smiling man with dark brown hair, dark suit, white shirt), Willem Mutsaerts (smiling man with silver-grey hair, dark suit), Christina Yeo (woman with dark bobbed hair and black jacket with pendant necklace, light blue-grey background), Fanny Iglesias (woman with long dark brown hair, black blazer, white top). Thin light grey horizontal rules above each profile. A white rectangle with thin teal outline at bottom right holds the statistics. Givaudan wordmark text at bottom center.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title black #000000 in Jost SemiBold about 5.2% of slide height; subtitle black Jost Regular about 5%. Names teal #35A2B6 in Jost SemiBold about 2.2%. Roles black Jost SemiBold about 1.84%; details black Jost Regular 1.84% with › markers. Bullets black Jost Regular 2.2% with bold key phrases. Stats teal Jost SemiBold 2.2%. Footer Jost Regular about 1.5%. Thin grey rules, teal 1px outline box, no shadows or gradients, square photos with no rounding.

**Layout**

Centered two-line title at top. Below, a 4-column by 2-row grid of board member cards, each with a teal name, black role and bullet lines at left and a square portrait at right, a thin grey rule above each card. Beneath the grid, a bullet list spans the left two-thirds and a thin-outlined teal-text statistics box sits at bottom right. Footer text bottom left, page number bottom right, Givaudan wordmark at bottom centre.

**Visuals**

Eight square colour portrait photographs of board members on light grey backgrounds, head-and-shoulders, smiling, in business attire: row 1 a bald man in a dark jacket (Gilles Andrier), a woman with short blonde hair and dark navy blazer (Ingrid Deltenre), an older white-haired man with glasses in a navy jacket (Victor Balli), a man with swept grey hair in a dark suit and white shirt (Louie D'Amico); row 2 a woman with short blonde-brown hair in a brown jacket (Melanie Maas-Brunner), a dark-haired woman with glasses and floral top (Sophie Gasperment), a bald man in navy jacket and white shirt (Roberto Guidetti), a woman with long wavy dark hair and a pearl necklace in black (Ester Baiget Arnau). Thin light grey horizontal rules above each card. A thin light grey outlined rectangle surrounds the statistics box. Black round bullet dots for the list. Givaudan wordmark text at bottom centre.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Titles black #000000 in Jost; main title SemiBold about 5.2% of slide height, subtitle Regular about 5%. Names teal #35A2B6 Jost SemiBold about 2.2%. Role and bullet text black Jost about 1.84%. Bottom bullet text Jost about 2%, with bold lead phrases. Statistics in teal #35A2B6 SemiBold. Thin grey lines, no shadows or gradients, square corners.

**Text**

Our strategy
2030 strategy
Driving sustainable growth with customers
Givaudan
Human by nature

**Layout**

Full-bleed photograph with all text centred horizontally. A small title sits at the top centre, the large title and subtitle sit at mid-height, and the Givaudan logo with its tagline sits at bottom centre.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed, warm, muted, grainy photograph of a Mediterranean seascape seen from behind a clothesline. The upper half is a hazy beige-grey sky. Misty blue-grey hills run along the horizon at about 35-48% of the height. Blue-grey sea fills the middle, with sparkling sun glitter on the right. A dark brown rocky outcrop juts in from the left edge at about 52-65% of the height. The bottom third is a foreground of laundry hanging on a line, held by wooden clothes pegs. From the left: a saturated red cloth, a peach floral sheet with orange and cream daisy patterns, a peach striped sheet in the centre, and a dark brown, orange and cream daisy-print fabric on the right. The foreground is soft-focus with warm side light. The white Givaudan wordmark logo with the tagline 'Human by nature' sits over the striped sheet at bottom centre.

**Design**

Background: Full-bleed photograph of a sea view with laundry on a clothesline, in muted beige, blue-grey and peach tones. Full-bleed warm-toned photo background, with no visible overlay beyond a slight haze. All text is white #FFFFFF in Jost, a geometric sans-serif. The top title and the large title are SemiBold; the subtitle is Regular. The large title is about 8.9% of the slide height, the subtitle about 4.4%, and the top title about 5.2%. No shapes, lines or shadows.

**Layout**

Full-bleed teal textured background with a large circular diagram centred (about x 18–82%), title centred at top over the circle. Left column 'Why' and right column 'Value creation' are centre-aligned text blocks with a thin white rule under each heading. Page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

A large circle crop of a photograph: a young woman with dark curly hair in a white collared shirt with small red embroidered flowers, face partly cropped at left, set against tall golden grasses in soft sunlight with blurred green and blue-sky background. Over the upper half is a teal semi-transparent half-ring ('What') with three white circles (Deepen geographical presence top centre, Strengthen core categories and optimise portfolio left, Extend customer & channel reach right) joined by thin white arcs. The lower half has a teal half-ring ('How') with three outlined white-stroke circles (Consumer-centric innovation left, Delivering value with excellence and agility bottom centre, Caring for people, nature and communities right) joined by thin white arcs. A thick white curved band at the bottom edge of the lower ring carries black curved uppercase text. In the centre a circular photo window of grasses holds the white bold text 'Driving sustainable growth with customers'. A thin white horizontal line crosses the circle at about y 50% and extends to both slide edges, curving around the ring. Thin white rules sit beneath 'Why' and 'Value creation'. The background is a teal-tinted leaf photograph.

**Design**

Background: Teal-tinted leaf photograph (#5FA5B5) full-bleed. Teal #5FA5B5 photographic background with leaf texture; white text for titles and body (#FFFFFF); black text (#000000) inside the white circles and on the curved band; white filled circles with outlined circles in the lower ring. Typeface Jost (geometric sans-serif); title SemiBold about 5.2% of slide height, centre text SemiBold 3.3%, headings SemiBold 2.6%, body Regular 2.6%, circle labels Regular 2.2%, footnote 1.5%. **Layout**

White slide with a centred title at top. Three equal columns (about 33% each) run edge to edge: a photo band, a teal chevron-shaped band with a centred heading, and centred body text below. Footer holds a left date line, a centred Givaudan wordmark and a right page number.

**Visuals**

Three full-width photos across the top of the columns, each with a downward-pointing chevron (V-shaped) bottom edge, separated from the teal bands by a thin white line. Centre: sunlit street in a Mexican-style town with yellow and blue-grey facades, stacks of straw hats on the pavement, a man browsing hanging souvenirs and a woman at right. Right: bright, blurred white lab scene with a gloved hand pouring amber liquid from a brown bottle into a glass test tube held by another gloved hand. Three teal bands sit under the photos, each with a V-shaped notch at the top and a pointed chevron bottom. The Givaudan wordmark is in the footer.

**Text**

Caring for people, nature and communities
Belonging together
Foster inclusion, increase transparency, and simplify processes for a better employee experience, strengthening Givaudan as the top choice for talent and a place where we all love to be and grow.
Growing together
Build winning teams and create personal career growth opportunities by enhancing leadership and management capabilities and investing in skills and expertise, digital adoption and literacy.
Safe together
Achieve best-in-class environment, health and safety by cultivating a culture of care, increasing personal accountability, ensuring compliance, and proactively managing EHS risks.
Sustainable together
Leverage sustainability to win more and defend existing business, co-develop more sustainable portfolios, and benefit nature and communities through partnerships across our supply chains.
Givaudan

### Slide 43

**Text**

Our purpose
Creating for happier, healthier
lives with love for nature.
Let’s imagine together.

**Layout**

Full-bleed photograph covering the slide. A small centred heading sits at the top centre. A three-line centred statement sits across the middle, over the lower part of the woman's face and the grasses.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed photograph, 1281 by 720 px, of a young woman with dark, curly shoulder-length hair and a fringe. She looks straight at the camera with a calm expression. She wears a pale grey-white loose top with a folded collar or scarf that has small red and yellow floral prints. She stands on the left third of the frame, cropped at the chest, in a meadow. Tall dry beige grasses are in the foreground, blurred and soft on the right. The background is blurred green trees under a pale blue sky. Daylight is soft and natural, the palette is muted greens, beiges and pale blue, and the focus is shallow. No overlay or tint is visible.

**Design**

Background: Full-bleed photograph of a woman in a meadow with grasses against a blue sky (underlying fill #35A2B6). Text only, in white Jost SemiBold (geometric sans-serif). The heading is about 5.2% of slide height and the statement about 8.9%. No shapes, lines or shadows are visible. The text sits directly on the photo.

### Slide 44

**Text**

Our purpose ambitions
Creating for happier, healthier lives with love for nature.
Let’s imagine together.
2030
Specific future focus
Nature
Let’s imagine together that we show our
love for nature in everything we do
Climate:
• We will reduce scope 1+2+3 GHG emissions in line with the
SBTi Net Zero Standard trajectory
Biodiversity:
• To contribute to protecting and regenerating biodiversity:
− We will source our critical agricultural commodities without
contributing to deforestation or natural ecosystem conversion
− We will source our key raw materials from supply chains
engaged in Regenerative Agriculture
2045
Climate:
• We will reach net-zero GHG emissions across our
value chain
People
Let's imagine together that Givaudan is a
place where we all love to be and grow
• 50% of our senior leaders will be women
• Our employees consistently rank Givaudan among the top
companies for inclusion and wellbeing*
2035
• We will reduce our total recordable case rate by
50% from our 2023 baseline
Communities
Let's imagine together that all communities
benefit by working with Givaudan
• We will source all materials and services in a way that protects
people and the environment: 100% sourced responsibly**
• We will improve the lives of millions of people in communities
where we source and operate
*This will be defined as achieving scores within the 75th percentile external
benchmark on our inclusion and wellbeing index, as measured through our
internal engagement surveys.
**% by procurement spend, flagged as sourced responsibly upon completion
of basic due diligence as defined in our Sourcing4Good programme.
2026 - Investor presentation – updated September 2026
Givaudan
44

**Layout**

Full-width teal photo banner across the top 24% holding a centred white title and two-line subtitle. Below, a three-column table (row labels at left, 2030 column in the middle, Specific future focus column at right) with three rows separated by thin black horizontal rules; footer with left date line, centred Givaudan wordmark and right page number.

**Visuals**

Top banner: close-up macro photograph of a leaf with fine veins, tinted teal/turquoise (about #3A94A8 with lighter #8BBFCB patches at upper left), full-bleed across the top 24% of the slide, cropped. Three thin black horizontal rules spanning x 3.3% to 96.6% (under the column headers at about y 33%, at about y 60%, and at about y 75%). Givaudan wordmark in the footer, centre, as black text logo.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF with a teal-tinted leaf photograph banner across the top 24%. White body background with teal leaf-photo header. Title white #FFFFFF Jost SemiBold about 5.2% of slide height; subtitle white Jost Regular about 3.7%. Row headings black #000000 Jost SemiBold about 3%, descriptions Jost Regular 2.2%, body bullets Jost Regular 2.0% with round bullets and en-dash sub-bullets, column headers Jost SemiBold 2.2%. Footnotes Jost Regular 1.5%. Thin black rules about 1px, no shadows or gradients.

### Slide 45

**Text**

Bringing our purpose to life
Nature
Let’s imagine together that we show our love for nature in everything we do
Climate
We will reach net-zero greenhouse gas (GHG) emissions across our value chain by 2045.
Water
Acting as a role model in water stewardship while contributing to SDGs 6, Clean Water and Sanitation for All and 12, Responsible Consumption and Production.
Waste and plastics
We will reach zero operational waste directed to landfill for all manufacturing sites by 2030.
We will decrease our operational waste for disposal intensity by 15% by 2030 (baseline 2020).
We will achieve 100% plastic circularity by 2030.
Biodiversity
We will contribute to protecting and regenerating biodiversity by 2030.
We will source our critical agricultural commodities without contributing to deforestation or natural ecosystem conversion.
We will source our key raw materials from supply chains engaged in Regenerative Agriculture.
Givaudan
2026 - Investor presentation – updated September 2026
45

**Layout**

White slide with centered two-line title and a centered bold tagline below. Four equal columns in a row, each with a photo on top, then a heading and body text beside a thin blue vertical rule. Footer has date text at left, centered Givaudan wordmark, and page number at right.

**Visuals**

Four photographs in a row, same size, no borders. 1) Climate: green forested mountain valley with misty clouds and a pale sky, dark green tones. 2) Water: water treatment basin with a steel truss bridge over calm water, a sunlit horizon, blue sky and reflections. 3) Waste and plastics: abstract close-up of yellow-olive leaves or butterflies seen through translucent crinkled plastic film. 4) Biodiversity: macro close-up of rock covered in green moss and white-grey lichen. Four thin blue vertical lines sit to the left of the column text, with differing lengths. Givaudan wordmark in the footer.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background. All text black #000000 in Jost, a geometric sans-serif. Titles and headings are SemiBold, body text is Regular. Title is about 5% of slide height, tagline about 3.7%, column headings about 3.3%, body about 2%. Accent is thin blue rules #1F78B4, about 2px wide. No shadows, gradients or rounded corners.

**Text**

Bringing our purpose to life
People
Let's imagine together that Givaudan is a place where we all love to be and grow
Everyone feels welcome, / valued and inspired

Caring for health, safety / and wellbeing

Building the leadership and / expertise of all our people
We will accelerate new leadership essentials, / anticipate and innovate to attract the workforce / of tomorrow as well as ensure the skills and / competencies for now and for the future.

Left photo: a crowd of African women in colourful clothing (orange, red, pink, teal, patterned wraps) standing and talking outdoors under trees; in the left foreground a woman carries a large blue-and-teal patterned bundle on her head; at right a woman in a red skirt and cream top sits on a ledge; warm natural daylight, green foliage background, wide rectangular crop. Right photo: a farmer in a pale blue bucket hat and dusty pink henley shirt, bending over and tending green crop plants in a field, a blade of grass in his mouth, blue-gloved hands, a hazy pale sky and flat horizon behind, soft daylight, wide rectangular crop. A thin vertical blue line separates the text columns. Givaudan wordmark in footer centre.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background. All text black #000000 in Jost geometric sans-serif; title SemiBold about 5.2% of slide height, subtitle Regular about 5%, tagline SemiBold 3.7%, column headings SemiBold 3.3%, body Regular 2.2% with key phrases in SemiBold, footer Regular 1.5%. 49

**Layout**

Full-bleed photograph covering the slide. A small white heading is centred near the top (y about 6-11%). A large two-line white title is centred in the lower-middle area (y about 45-67%). A small Givaudan logo is centred at the bottom (y about 91-96%). A tiny page number sits at the bottom right (x about 95.7%, y 93%).

**Visuals**

Full-bleed dark, moody photograph of a baker in a cream/white button-up shirt and beige linen apron, shown from chest to waist with the head cropped out. Both hands hold a metal mesh sieve at the upper centre, and the right hand taps its rim. White flour falls in a cloud from the sieve into a pale grey-blue ceramic bowl at the bottom centre. Eggs, including a speckled quail egg, sit in a dark bowl at the bottom left. A grey cloth and another bowl appear at the bottom right. Lighting is low-key and warm on the hands and shirt, with a near-black background at the left and right edges. Dominant colours are black, cream, taupe and white. A small white Givaudan wordmark sits at the bottom centre over the bowl.

**Design**

Background: Full-bleed dark photograph of a baker sifting flour into a bowl, mostly black #0E0B0A at the edges. Dark photographic background with white text. Title and heading are in Jost SemiBold, white #FFFFFF, geometric sans-serif. The title is about 10% of slide height and the heading about 5%. Footer text is small and white. The page number is tiny and black #000000. There are no shapes, lines or shadows.

51

**Layout**

Centered two-line title at top on white. Below, four equal light-grey cards side by side with narrow gaps, each with left-aligned heading, description, and two progress bars (2020 above 2025) in the lower half. Footer: left-aligned text at bottom left, Givaudan wordmark at bottom centre, page number at bottom right; footnote inside the third card.

**Visuals**

Four light grey rectangular cards. Each card holds two horizontal progress bars with thin teal outline, rounded corners and white unfilled track. 2020 bars have pale teal fill (#7DCCDA) with value text right-aligned inside the bar; 2025 bars have darker teal fill (#35A2B6) with bolder values. Fill lengths: card 1 2020 about 32% of the track and 2025 about 51%; card 2 2020 about 85% and 2025 100%; card 3 2020 about 23% and 2025 about 87%; card 4 2020 about 25% and 2025 about 34%. Value text colours: pale teal on white track where the fill is short, white where the text sits on fill. Givaudan wordmark text logo at bottom centre.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background with #F2F2F2 cards. Title black #000000 in Jost SemiBold, subtitle Jost Regular, about 5% of slide height each. Card headings Jost SemiBold about 3% height in black; descriptions Jost Regular about 2.6%. Teal accents #7DCCDA and #35A2B6. Bars have 2px teal outlines and rounded corners, no shadows or gradients. Geometric sans-serif throughout.

**Layout**

Centered two-line title at top. Left column (about 3% to 61% wide) holds a bold lead sentence, a paragraph, a thin blue divider, a teal subheading and a row of five KPI figures separated by vertical rules. Right column (about 67% to 97%) holds a teal heading, five numbered circles with text, and a teal call-to-action box. Footer has the left-aligned source line, the centred Givaudan wordmark and the right-aligned page number 52.

**Visuals**

Five outlined circles containing the numbers 1 to 5, stacked vertically on the right, each with a blue (#006EA3) outline and number. A thin blue horizontal divider line under the paragraph on the left. Four thin blue vertical divider lines between the five KPI columns. A teal rectangle holding a white line-art open-book icon on its left. Givaudan wordmark text in the footer centre.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title black (#000000) in Jost SemiBold, subtitle Jost Regular. Teal accent (#35A2B6) for headings and big KPI numbers; blue (#006EA3) for circle outlines, numbers, and divider lines. Body text is black Jost Regular. KPI labels are Jost SemiBold black. Teal call-to-action box has white text with the link underlined. Flat style, no shadows or gradients, square corners on the box.

**Layout**

Two-part slide: a full-height photo panel on the left third (about 32% wide) holding the white headline, quote and Givaudan wordmark, and a white content area on the right with a centred title, a KPI block, a thin divider, and two columns (Taste & Wellbeing, Fragrance & Beauty) each with a headline figure, two stat boxes and a graphic. Footnote bottom-left of the content area, page number bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Left panel: photograph of calm dark blue sea with gentle ripples and a single thin wooden pole rising from the water at centre, soft pinkish-violet haze near the top fading to deep navy at the bottom; full-bleed on the left edge, cropped to the panel. Right: a solid teal rectangle containing 3,799 million CHF; four outlined teal-bordered boxes with big-number stats; two thin black horizontal rules above the column headings; a thin black vertical divider between the columns; two outlined teal rectangles with 1,789 and 2,010 totals; four small outlined teal stat boxes (two per column); a teal silhouette world map with regional percentages placed around it (Europe top, North America left, Latin America bottom left, SAMEA bottom, Asia Pacific right); a cluster of four teal flower icons (one large, two medium, one small) with percentages placed around them. Givaudan wordmark in white at the bottom of the photo panel.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF on the right; left 32% is a blue sea photograph. White background on the right with a dark blue photo on the left. Titles black #000000 in Jost SemiBold (geometric sans-serif), about 5.2% of slide height; the left headline white, same size. Body quote in white Jost Regular about 2.6% of height, centred. Accent teal #35A2B6 for the big numbers (Jost SemiBold, 3-4.6% of height), the % signs set smaller, and for the word 'sales'. Labels in black Jost Regular about 1.5%. Boxes have thin 1px teal outlines with square corners; no shadows or gradients.

### Slide 54

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Plots dividend per share against free cash flow over 26 years in one stacked bar chart, making a long financial history readable at a glance.
- Review: Slide 7 packs three charts and many tiny figures without a takeaway, and slide 54 clips the last two bar labels and has a title that does not match the free cash flow bars.

**Layout**

Centered two-line title at the top; a full-width combo chart (columns plus line) fills the middle with the legend centred beneath it. Footer: left-aligned presentation line at bottom left, Givaudan wordmark centred at the bottom, page number at bottom right; footnote right-aligned above the footer.

**Visuals**

Combo chart of 26 years (2000 to 2025) with no printed y-axis. Teal columns show Free cash flow with white bold labels inside the top of each bar: 2000 203, 2001 340, 2002 273, 2003 295, 2004 390, 2005 289, 2006 262, 2007 157, 2008 145, 2009 549, 2010 437, 2011 117, 2012 512, 2013 662, 2014 604, 2015 720, 2016 597, 2017 594, 2018 703, 2019 787, 2020 811, 2021 843, 2022 479, 2023 920, 2024 1,158, 2025 1,053. An orange line with diamond markers shows Dividend with black labels above each point: 6.5, 7, 8.1, 15.4, 16.3, 17.6, 18.8, 19.5, 20, 20.6, 21.5, 22, 36, 47, 50, 54, 56, 58, 60, 62, 64, 66, 67, 68, 70, 72*. The line rises steeply in 2003 and 2012 and then flattens. Grey horizontal baseline axis with year labels beneath each bar, and a thin grey vertical axis line at the right edge. Legend: teal square for Free cash flow, orange line with diamond for Dividend. The Givaudan wordmark is in the footer.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white background. Titles in black #000000 in Jost: bold/semibold headline and regular subtitle, with 'th' as superscript. Teal #35A2B6 bars, orange #F07000 line and markers, white bold bar labels, black small labels. Geometric sans-serif (Jost) throughout. Title about 5.2% of slide height, subtitle 5%, data labels about 1.7-1.8%, footer about 1.5%. No shadows or gradients.

### Slide 56

**Text**

2026 Outlook
Business context
Navigating a volatile geopolitical landscape and uncertain market conditions
Strong natural hedges across product segments, geographies and customer groups
2026 outlook
Strong focus on our customers and on maintaining our supply chain performance at a high level
Input cost impact estimated at low single-digit percentage for H2 2026
Pricing actions are being taken to fully compensate for incremental input costs
US tariffs partially recovered, will be repaid accordingly to customers in the second half of 2026, impacting LFL sales growth
Givaudan
56

**Layout**

Two-part slide: a full-height photo occupies the left 40% of the slide; the right 60% is white with a centred title near the top and left-aligned text in two sections (Business context, 2026 outlook) separated by a thin teal horizontal rule. The Givaudan wordmark sits at the bottom of the right area and the page number at the bottom-right corner.

**Visuals**

Full-height, full-bleed left-edge photo: extreme macro close-up of a leaf's vein network, a web of polygonal cells outlined by pale, light veins, tinted monochrome teal/turquoise (about #5BAAB8 to #7FC0CB) with soft, even light and slightly lighter vein lines. Cropped to a 40% by 100% panel. A thin teal horizontal divider line sits between the two text sections. The text 'Givaudan' acts as the logo wordmark at the bottom.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF, with the teal-tinted leaf photo covering the left 40%. White background on the right. Title in black #000000 Jost SemiBold (geometric sans-serif, Futura-like), about 5.2% of slide height. Section headings Jost SemiBold black at about 3% height; body bullets Jost Regular black at about 3% height with small round bullet dots (Arial). The divider is a thin teal line (#4A9FB5), about 1px. The photo has a teal monochrome tint. No shadows or rounded corners.

**Layout**

Full-bleed photograph with all text centred horizontally. The title sits just below the vertical middle over the mountain horizon, the subtitle directly beneath it, and the word Givaudan is centred near the bottom. No page number or footer.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed photograph of a calm lake at dusk. Dusky blue-grey sky fills the top with small pink-tinted clouds, and a larger band of salmon-pink cloud sits above a dark blue-grey mountain range on the horizon at about 55 to 58% of the height. A dark strip of low tree-covered hills runs along the far shore at about 57 to 58% of the height. The lower 40% is rippled water in muted slate blue and purple-grey. Soft, low light with a slight dark tint over the image and a vignette at the corners. The photograph is not boxed or cropped inside a frame.

**Design**

Background: Full-bleed dusk photograph of a lake with mountains and pink clouds, tinted darker grey-blue (#6F7C8E average). Muted dusk photograph as background, slightly darkened for legibility. White text throughout. Title in a geometric sans-serif (Jost SemiBold), about 10% of slide height. Subtitle and Givaudan in Jost Regular, about 4.4% of slide height. No lines, shapes, shadows or gradients are visible.

### Slide 60

**Text**

Follow us on social media @givaudan
Givaudan
Human by nature

**Layout**

Single centred column on a white slide: the heading sits just below the vertical middle, a row of five social icons directly beneath it, and the Givaudan wordmark with its tagline centred near the bottom. No page number or footer.

**Visuals**

Row of five black rounded-square social media icons, left to right: LinkedIn (white 'in'), Twitter (white bird), Facebook (white 'f'), Instagram (white camera outline), YouTube (white play button). Below is the black Givaudan wordmark logo with the tagline 'Human by nature' under it.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white slide with black text and icons only. Heading in Jost SemiBold, about 4.4% of slide height, black #000000. Logo wordmark in a light geometric sans (Futura-like), tagline in a small geometric sans. Icons are solid black rounded squares with white glyphs; no shadows, gradients or other accents.
